## 🤔 What is GPT Image 2?

**GPT Image 2** (codename **"duct-tape"**) is OpenAI's next-generation image model. Community testing highlights a leap in these areas:

- 🎯 **Pixel-Perfect Text Rendering** — Native-quality text in Chinese, English, and Japanese, no typos or warped glyphs
- 🎨 **Cross-Image Consistency** — The same character, style, or IP stays identical across a series, down to the pixel
- ⚡ **Commercial-Grade Illustration** — Illustration output is ready to ship without manual polish
- 🌈 **True Art Style Induction** — Creatively evokes the feeling of a style rather than merely approximating references
- 🔧 **Storyboard & Product Series** — Ideal for storyboards, IP characters, and multi-panel product visuals
- 📐 **Multi-Language Design** — Social cards, banners, and posters with accurate multilingual typography in one shot

📚 **Learn More:** See community testing in our [report summary](docs/FAQ.md)

### 🚀 Raycast Integration

Some prompts support **dynamic arguments** using [Raycast Snippets](https://raycast.com/help/snippets) syntax. Look for the 🚀 Raycast Friendly badge!

**Example:**
```
A quote card with "{argument name="quote" default="Stay hungry, stay foolish"}"
by {argument name="author" default="Steve Jobs"}
```

When used in Raycast, you can dynamically replace the arguments for quick iterations!

### No. 1: VR Headset Exploded View Poster

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Generates a high-tech exploded view diagram of a VR headset with detailed component callouts and promotional text.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
  "type": "exploded view product diagram poster",
  "subject": "VR headset",
  "style": "clean high-tech 3D render, studio lighting, glowing accents",
  "background": "{argument name=\"background color\" default=\"soft purple and blue gradient\"}",
  "header": {
    "logo": "∞ {argument name=\"product name\" default=\"Meta Quest 3\"}",
    "subtitle": "{argument name=\"main catchphrase\" default=\"まったく新しい現実を、まったく新しい構造から。\"}"
  },
  "layout": {
    "centerpiece": "vertically stacked exploded view of a VR headset showing 9 distinct layers of internal components: outer shell, camera sensors, motherboard with chip, pancake lenses, internal frame, battery packs, side straps, top strap, and facial interface cushion.",
    "callout_labels": {
      "count": 8,
      "left_side": [
        "Snapdragon® XR2 Gen 2\n圧倒的な処理性能でリアルタイムな体験を。",
        "調整可能なIPD機構\n幅広いユーザーに快適なフィット感を。",
        "精密設計されたヘッドストラップ\n快適さと安定性を追求したエルゴノミクス。"
      ],
      "right_side": [
        "フェイスプレート\n洗練されたデザインと最適な重量バランス。",
        "トラッキングカメラ\n高精度な位置トラッキングと環境認識を実現。",
        "パンケーキレンズ\n薄型設計で広い視野角と鮮明な映像を提供。",
        "高性能バッテリー\n長時間駆動を支える最適化された電源設計。",
        "柔らかなフェイスインターフェース\n長時間でも快適な装着感を実現。"
      ]
    },
    "footer": {
      "left_text_block": {
        "headline": "{argument name=\"bottom headline\" default=\"体験は、構造から進化する。\"}",
        "body": "一つひとつのパーツに、没入体験を支える最先端テクノロジーとこだわりの設計。Meta Quest 3は、未来を感じさせる体験を内部から生み出しています。"
      },
      "right_logo": "∞ Meta"
    }
  }
}
```

### No. 2: Illustrated City Food Map

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Generates a hand-drawn, watercolor-style tourist map featuring numbered local food specialties, landmarks, and a legend.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
  "type": "illustrated map infographic",
  "style": "{argument name=\"art style\" default=\"watercolor and ink hand-drawn illustration on vintage parchment\"}",
  "title_section": {
    "text": "{argument name=\"city name\" default=\"成都\"} {argument name=\"map title\" default=\"吃货暴走地图\"}",
    "mascot": "cartoon red chili pepper wearing sunglasses and giving a thumbs up"
  },
  "border": "{argument name=\"border decoration\" default=\"vine of green leaves and red chili peppers\"}",
  "layout": {
    "background": "textured beige parchment paper with yellow roads, blue rivers, and green park areas",
    "sections": [
      {
        "title": "landmarks",
        "count": 6,
        "illustrations": ["traditional pavilion", "traditional monastery", "modern skyscraper with climbing panda", "tall TV tower", "traditional gate", "industrial buildings"],
        "labels": ["人民公园", "文殊院", "IFS", "339电视塔", "宽窄巷子", "东郊记忆"]
      },
      {
        "title": "food_spots",
        "count": 12,
        "illustrations": ["mapo tofu", "dumplings in chili oil", "skewers in pot", "sticky rice balls", "egg baking cake", "nine-grid hotpot", "sweet potato noodles", "cold skewers", "spicy mixed dish", "covered tea bowl", "ice jelly dessert", "spicy rabbit heads"],
        "labels": ["1 陈麻婆豆腐", "2 钟水饺", "3 春熙路", "4 宽窄巷子·三大炮", "5 建设路·叶婆婆蛋烘糕", "6 玉林路·小龙坎火锅", "7 香香巷·肥肠粉", "8 武侯祠大街·钵钵鸡", "9 东郊记忆·冒椒火辣", "10 人民公园·鹤鸣茶社", "11 锦里古街·冰粉", "12 双流老妈兔头"]
      },
      {
        "title": "图例",
        "position": "bottom-right",
        "count": 5,
        "items": ["red dot", "green house", "green tree", "blue line", "yellow double line"],
        "labels": ["美食地点", "地标景点", "公园绿地", "河流湖泊", "主要道路"]
      }
    ],
    "centerpiece": "giant panda sitting and eating bamboo",
    "bottom_right_extras": ["vintage compass rose with N, S, E, W", "disclaimer text '温馨提示：吃辣需谨慎，肠胃要保护~' with a red chili pepper icon"]

### No. 4: E-commerce Live Stream UI Mockup

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Generates a realistic social media live stream interface overlaying a portrait, featuring customizable chat messages, gift popups, and a product purchase card.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
{
  "type": "live stream UI mockup",
  "subject": {
    "description": "portrait of {argument name=\"host name\" default=\"Elon Musk\"}, smiling, wearing a black t-shirt with a white technical schematic graphic",
    "background": "left side shows a screen with '{argument name=\"left background logo\" default=\"SPACEX\"}' text, right side shows a red '{argument name=\"right background logo\" default=\"Tesla T logo\"}' and a dark car"
  },
  "ui_overlay": {
    "top_header": {
      "host_info": "avatar, name '{argument name=\"host name\" default=\"Elon Musk\"}', subtext '55.6万本场点赞', red '关注' button",
      "rank_badge": "gold coin icon with '全站第1名'",
      "viewer_stats": "3 top viewer avatars with '12.3w', '8.6w', '5.7w', total '68.7万', 'X' close button",
      "right_links": "'更多直播 >', '礼物展馆 0/24' with blue '经典' tag"
    },
    "mid_left_gifts": {
      "count": 2,
      "items": [
        "avatar '科技爱好者', '送小心心', heart icon x 1314",
        "avatar '星辰大海', '送火箭', rocket icon x 666"
      ]
    },
    "bottom_left_chat": {
      "system_message": "level 37 badge '宇宙漫游者 加入了直播间'",
      "message_count": 7,
      "messages": [
        "小火箭: 马斯克！未来可期！🚀",
        "future: 特斯拉Model 2什么时候出？",
        "星空梦想家: SpaceX今年能上火星吗？",
        "AI探索者: Neuralink进展如何？",
        "帅气的网友: 马总好！",
        "Mars: 第一次来你的直播，超激动！",
        "用户123: 讲讲AI吧，会取代人类吗？"
      ]
    },
    "bottom_right_product_card": {
      "hot_tag": "orange '热卖 x 1888'",
      "image": "Tesla Cybertruck",
      "title": "{argument name=\"product name\" default=\"特斯拉Cybertruck 电动皮卡\"}",
      "price": "{argument name=\"product price\" default=\"¥ 1,618,000\"}",
      "button": "red '抢' button",
      "floating_animation": "translucent hearts floating up the right edge"
    },
    "bottom_bar": {
      "input_field": "'说点什么...'",
      "icons": ["smiley face", "three dots", "shopping cart", "gift box", "share"]
    }
  }
}
```

### No. 5: Anime Martial Arts Battle Illustration

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Featured](https://img.shields.io/badge/⭐-Featured-gold)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Generates a dynamic, high-impact anime illustration of two female characters fighting in a traditional dojo with elemental energy effects.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
An anime-style illustration of a {argument name="action type" default="high-impact martial arts battle"} between two young female fighters in a {argument name="setting" default="traditional wooden martial arts dojo"}. In the foreground, a girl with black hair in a high bun wears a {argument name="character 1 color theme" default="red and white"} Chinese-style martial arts outfit with baggy pants. She is in a dynamic, low, forward-thrusting stance, surrounded by swirling red energy and water splashes. - {argument name="character aesthetic" default="Korean/Japanese manga-inspired aesthetic"}

- Long flowing dark hair

Character integration:

- Add realistic interaction with the environment through natural shadows and overlapping perspective
- Keep the background completely photographic and detailed
- Do not turn the background into anime or illustration

For the outfit, use casual modern streetwear appropriate for the location. Preserve recognizable clothing details such as oversized T-shirts, jeans, sneakers, shoulder bag, jersey numbers, or accessories when specified in the reference image.

Composition:

- Character positioned naturally in the foreground or middle ground
- Keep the original landmark, stadium, buildings, stairs, trees, sky, advertisements, and surroundings clearly visible
- Maintain the original photograph's wide-angle composition
- Bright natural daylight
- Crisp details
- Realistic photographic background
- Strong contrast between the monochrome illustrated character and colorful real environment

Overall aesthetic: cinematic travel photography + Korean street photography + monochrome manga line art + scrapbook/sticker illustration, with seamless perspective and lighting integration.

Do not make the entire image cartoon-like. Only the character should be illustrated; everything else must remain photorealistic.

### No. 31: Social Media Post - Dunhuang Papercut Dreamscape Art

![Language-ZH](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-ZH-blue)

#### 📖 Description

A detailed prompt for creating a 3D layered papercut art piece inspired by Dunhuang's Mogao Caves. It features an Oriental woman entering a massive, intricately carved paper sculpture cave, with vibrant colors like peacock blue and sand gold.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
9:16 vertical version, modern oriental female dreamscape dimensional papercut art, modern oriental papercut art, layered paper cut illustration, dimensional paper sculpture, intricate paper carving, paper diorama, poetic female dreamscape. The theme of the work is 'She walks into the unfinished page of a Dunhuang mural.' A visual 24-year-old oriental adult female, mysterious, elegant, quiet and determined, walking alone along long steps in a paper-carved cave. The character has a clear oval face, slender almond eyes slightly raised, and a quiet, focused expression. Long black hair is half-draped and half-tied, with strands naturally connecting to Feitian-style paper ribbons, wearing light gold floating clouds and small flower branch hair accessories. The character wears a multi-layered paper art long dress composed of ochre, stone green, peacock blue and gold. The hem features Feitian, caisson, sand patterns and Crescent Spring-style hollow patterns. The outer layer of silk ribbons flutters lightly behind her in the wind. The clothing has clear paper thickness, fiber texture, cut edges and dense hollow structures. The scene uses a relatively simple multi-layered stage-style composition, not crowded with mural characters. The main subject is a huge, nested Dunhuang-style paper-carved cave entrance. The arched door frame occupies the main space of the picture, with a large caisson paper-carved pattern at the top as a visual anchor. Only a few Feitian paper carvings and mural character silhouettes are kept on the left and right of the door, creating a sense of a dream of civilization, but avoiding complex crowding. The character is located in a small position at the lower center of the screen, facing away or two-thirds side-facing away from the camera, walking towards the huge door along several wide paper art steps. The scale of the character is significantly smaller than the door to emphasize the massive scale of the cave. Through the door, you can directly see another opened Dunhuang dream: vast golden sand dunes, curved blue water like the Crescent Spring, distant mountains, paper art clouds and a thin moon in the sky. The dunes and water surface are formed by multiple layers of paper, and the space is open and clean, without too many buildings. The overall colors are brighter and clearer than traditional Dunhuang colors: dominated by high-saturation but high-end peacock blue, stone green, ochre and warm sand gold, with an appropriate amount of cinnabar orange-red as a visual highlight. The outer layer of the door uses ochre and gold, the middle layer adds stone green and peacock blue, the distant desert uses warm gold and light ochre, and the Crescent Spring uses a bright blue-green. Colors are clearly separated from each other, without a uniform brownish-yellow filter, not gray or dirty. The lighting uses a transition from warm inside the cave to brighter outside. Golden celestial light shines from the direction of the distant dunes and Crescent Spring, passing through the paper-carved door and falling on the steps and the character's silhouette, forming a clear but soft guiding light. The edges of the caisson, Feitian, door frame and character's ribbons have a delicate translucent feel, while the dark areas still maintain layers of peacock blue, stone green and ochre. All caves, caissons, Feitian, sand dunes, Crescent Spring, clouds, characters and clothing must be unified into a high-level dimensional paper carving language. Strengthen the real paper fibers, paper layer thickness, hand-cut edges, slight creases and inter-layer projections. The whole piece is like a high-end Dunhuang-themed paper carving art installation, rather than a common mural illustration. The overall mood is: ancient, mysterious, quiet, vast, civilization is reawakening. The focus of the picture is 'Behind a huge doorway, another page of the ancient world is reopened', the composition is simple and powerful, the character is small, the space is large, and the colors are bright and transparent. Negative prompt: real-life photography, ordinary anime, plastic 3D, resin, clay, felt, children's handmade, ordinary Dunhuang tourism illustration, too many mural characters, accumulated decorations, overcrowded screen, oversized characters, large number of Buddha statues stacked, cave too dark, brownish-yellow filter, overall gray, dirty colors, saturation too low, dead black in shadows, inconspicuous Crescent Spring, sand dune space too small, doorway too small, messy paper layers, blurred paper edges, material separation between character and background, text, Logo, watermark.

### No. 37: Infographic / Edu Visual - Watercolor Isometric Deconstruction Poster

![Language-ZH](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-ZH-blue)

#### 📖 Description

A sophisticated prompt that deconstructs an uploaded image into an isometric watercolor study, styled like an academic or editorial research poster.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Process similarly:

Please transform each photo I upload into an independent high-end design poster, outputting each photo individually without multi-image stitching. The overall composition should use a 3:4 vertical layout, with the upper and lower areas split strictly 1:1, each occupying 50% of the frame.

The upper part retains the original photo, maintaining the subject's identity, structure, proportions, perspective, orientation, realism, natural lighting, and original color atmosphere, with only slight high-end photographic color grading to give it the texture of an art magazine, independent publication, or exhibition photography. To fit the frame, the sky, ground, or environmental background can be naturally extended, but the subject must not be stretched, distorted, or changed, and no other objects should be mixed in or original content replaced.

The lower part extracts the most recognizable subject, outline, pose, and narrative relationship from the photo, reconstructing it as a structural deconstruction watercolor study following isometric logic. The uploaded original photo is the sole basis; the subject's identity, geometric form, proportions, observation direction, spatial relationship, primary colors, and scale references must come only from the original image. Do not paste the photo directly, and do not create before-and-after comparisons, collages, or photographic slices.

When redrawing, first identify the core subject, main axes, original observation direction, foreground/midground/background relationships, 3–7 most important identifying features, 4–6 primary colors, and environmental cues that reflect scale. Retain the subject's overall silhouette, general proportions, original perspective, symmetrical or asymmetrical relationships, key component relationships, identity-defining details, and primary material colors, so that it is instantly recognizable as the same object from the upper half.

The subject adopts isometric redrawing logic, reorganized with clear, restrained, and professional axonometric spatial expression, while remaining faithful to the original photo's structural relationships and viewing logic. Based on the subject's actual structure, deconstruct it into 2–5 meaningful complete levels or components: buildings, vessels, or combined objects can be deconstructed along roofs, floors, shells, frames, bases, modules, or connectors; landscapes or spaces can be unfolded by foreground, midground, background, and spatial enclosure layers; humans, animals, or plants should remain physically whole, expressed only through overlapping outlines, pose layers, action lines, and surface color blocks—dismemberment, dissection, or mechanical partitioning is prohibited. All layers should be separated with restraint along real axes or perspective directions, with uniform and moderate spacing, sharing the same central axis and spatial grid, and retaining one complete, stable, and most detailed base or anchor area so the audience can reassemble the subject in their mind.

The lower part's composition remains professional, restrained, and editorial. The subject is centered or slightly offset according to the original photo's center of gravity, with ample white space around it. The stable base is located at the bottom or center, and the separated structural layers expand upward, outward, or toward the depth along real axes. Avoid dramatic fragmentation, excessive exploded views, meaningless decoration, and mechanical disassembly. The overall look should be a highly finished research-oriented editorial poster rather than an engineering manual.

The painting language adopts the character of an architectural structural deconstruction watercolor study on a warm ivory watercolor paper background, combining clear line work, semi-transparent watercolor blocks, a few structural auxiliary lines, and restrained analytical markings. Retain slight paper grain, detailed brushstrokes, transparent layered colors, and rational white space to give it both the intellectual feel of a research diagram and the elegance of an art publication. Colors are extracted from the original photo and appropriately purified, remaining clear, accurate, restrained, and spirited, avoiding gray, muddy, heavy, over-rendered, or cheap CG effects.

Text naturally intervenes as part of the composition in the manner of research diagrams/editorial annotations. Extract a short title from the subject's identity, theme, action, state, or symbolic meaning from the photo, and pair it with a minimal amount of numbering, structural markers, lateral annotations, material words, state words, or miniature descriptions. The text remains slender, restrained, and professional, arranged horizontally, vertically, or in offset patterns along axes, layering directions, white space edges, or structural annotation positions, making it look like a blend of architectural research, watercolor observation, and high-end editorial design rather than a standard poster title.

The overall presentation features isometric structural logic, professional deconstructive order, watercolor study texture, editorial white space, and a high-end publishing vibe. Whether the subject is architecture, humans, animals, plants, objects, vehicles, street scenes, interiors, or natural landscapes, the lower half should grow the most appropriate deconstruction method from the original photo's own structure, maintaining high adaptability and portability. Avoid templated exploded views, complex tech UIs, feelings of dismemberment, mechanical parts, cartoonishness, e-commerce vibes, and unfounded structural fabrications.

Maintain a balance between the real subject, core meaning, micro-narrative, naive lines, and a large amount of white space. Regardless of whether the original image is a person, animal, plant, food, building, object, vehicle, or natural scene, first grasp "what this photo is truly about" before deciding how to reconstruct it, avoiding just capturing the object without the relationship, doing cute interactions without a theme, cartoon sticker vibes, complex decoration, and a templated feel.
```

### No. 38: Infographic / Edu Visual - Da Vinci Isometric Structural Poster

![Language-ZH](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-ZH-blue)

#### 📖 Description

A complex design prompt that combines isometric 3D reconstruction with Renaissance-style technical sketches for a scientific research aesthetic.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Please turn each photo I upload into an individual high-end design poster, no multi-image stitching, each photo output separately. The overall composition adopts a 3:4 portrait format, with the top and bottom areas strictly 1:1 in height, each occupying 50% of the frame.

The upper part retains the original photo, maintaining the main structure, real texture, natural light/shadow, and original color atmosphere, with only slight professional photographic color grading to give it an art magazine, independent publication, or exhibition photography feel. To fit the aspect ratio, the sky, ground, or environmental background can be naturally extended, but the subject must not be stretched, distorted, or changed.

The lower part extracts the most recognizable **subject, silhouette, posture, and narrative relationship** from the photo, reconstructed with **isometric spatial logic**. It is not simply turning the original object into a 3D model, but dismantling the subject into clear blocks, levels, sections, and spatial relationships, recombining them in a unified isometric axis to have both the structural order of an architectural model and the visual generalization of an editorial illustration. The viewing angle, scale, and local structure can be moderately adjusted to better showcase the most representative form of the subject, but it must remain instantly recognizable.

Simultaneously, extract a **thematic image** from the core content, action, structure, or symbolic relationship of the photo, geometrizing and symbolizing it to integrate into the isometric composition. The thematic image can serve as a base, a section, a spatial gap, a path, an enclosing structure, or a negative shape, allowing the subject to interact through penetration, fitting, suspension, cutting, support, or staggered layering. The theme is not an additional decoration but a compositional mechanism that determines how the subject exists in space.

The composition is adaptively designed based on the visual center of gravity, silhouette trend, and spatial characteristics of the subject, establishing a professional order through isometric axes, block proportions, hierarchical staggering, scale contrast, local cropping, and large areas of negative space. The subject does not need to be mechanically centered; it can be offset, suspended, or extended, so that the isometric structure and thematic image together form the sole visual center.

The subject uses **high-brightness, clear, and soft Japanese-style color palettes with delicate matte materials**. Colors are extracted and modulated from the most vibrant and representative spirits of the subject in the photo above, using soft white, cream white, or very light warm tones as the base, converting core colors into clean, bright, soft planes, distinguishing different blocks through tonal variations and minimal accent colors. Lighting is soft yet clear, using slight light and shadow to help read the structure, avoiding heavy 3D shadows, plastic materials, and muddy tones.

The background maintains a large amount of white space and incorporates **Leonardo da Vinci-style creative research marks**: silhouette sketches related to the subject, structural dismantling, partial enlargements, proportion lines, measurement lines, arrows, geometric deductions, natural observation line drawings, and micro-annotations, faintly distributed around the subject in pale pencil, ochre ink, or faded manuscript texture. The manuscript should be like thought tracks left by the design process, contrasting the precise isometric subject as 'rational product vs. emotional research process,' without forming a second visual center.

Text is also organized around the theme and spatial structure. Extract a short English title from the subject's identity, action, emotion, or symbolic meaning of the photo, paired with a minimal amount of location, status words, or micro-annotations. Text can be arranged along isometric axes, thematic image boundaries, structural sections, or manuscript auxiliary lines, in horizontal, vertical, staggered, interspersed, or hidden in negative shapes, making the text look like an information layer in design drawings and high-end art publications rather than an afterthought title.

The overall presentation features a visual temperament of **Thematic Metaphor × Isometric Spatial Order × Da Vinci-style Research Manuscript × Japanese Clear Colors × Eastern Negative Space × High-end Editorial Design**. Whether the subject is a person, animal, plant, building, object, food, vehicle, or natural landscape, it should find the most suitable isometric reconstruction method based on its own structure, avoiding ordinary 3D models, game scene vibes, axonometric engineering drawing vibes, cartoon low-poly, mechanical centering, e-commerce display vibes, and template-like feel.
```

Order Value”, “+12% vs Q1”; 4) donut chart icon, “24.8%”, “Gross Margin”, “+6.3pp vs Q1”. Below, include exactly 1 line chart labeled “GROWTH TREND” with “Revenue (USD)” and month labels JAN, FEB, MAR, APR, MAY, JUN. To the right, include exactly 4 growth driver rows: “Product Innovation”, “Marketing Efficiency”, “Sales Expansion”, and “Customer Retention”, each with a small amber icon. Inside the card, use monospaced white text reading: “Create a clean infographic for [TOPIC] with accurate labels, metrics, icons, arrows, and chart elements. Leave visual breathing room. Then convert it into a HyperFrames plan that animates count-ups, line draws, arrows, highlights, captions, and voiceover timing in a deterministic 9:16 or 16:9 MP4.”

Footer: Bottom left text “@DSIVMUSIC”. Bottom right small circular glyph resembling a stylized “5”.

Visual style: Cinematic black-on-black product photography mixed with crisp UI design, sharp typography, subtle glow, amber highlights, accurate readable text, realistic monitor lighting, clean information hierarchy.

Constraints: Keep all text legible and spelled exactly as specified. Use exactly 2 benefit rows, 4 metric cards, 4 growth driver rows, and 4 process boxes. Do not add extra logos, watermarks, or unrelated text.
```

Keep warm window light, cream hanfu fabric with subtle embroidered floral details, and a more smooth, influencer-like face.

Bottom panel: Label “Seedream 5.0 Pro”. Make this brighter and slightly more plastic/smooth than the top, with a gentle smile and softer realism. The woman holds the peach flower close to her ear, looking down-left or sideways, with intense backlight from the lattice window and a glowing edge around her hair and hands.

Text styling: Large white text, simple sans-serif, no outline, subtle shadow if needed for readability. Place each label consistently near the bottom-left of its panel. Do not add any other text, logos, watermarks, or UI elements.

Customizable details: The subject is {argument name="subject description" default="a young East Asian woman in ivory hanfu"}; the setting is {argument name="setting" default="a warm ancient Chinese room with wooden lattice windows"}; the hair accessory is {argument name="hair flower" default="a peach-pink flower"}; the top label is {argument name="top model label" default="Midjourney V8.2"}; the middle label is {argument name="middle model label" default="GPT-Image 2"}.

Canvas: Portrait poster, 2:3 ratio, dark textured black-brown background with subtle grunge, aged paper feel, red and gold accents, dramatic warm food photography lighting.

Layout: At the top left, place a small red square seal containing vertical Japanese text 「本場の麻婆をお家で」. Across the top, set a cream brush-style tagline 「辛さ・痺れ・旨みの三位一体。」 with a thin horizontal rule underneath and a small gold cloud ornament in the top right. The center is dominated by an enormous red calligraphy headline 「陳麻婆豆腐」, with a smaller red seal on the right containing 「陳」. Beneath the headline, add the subtitle 「— 本格四川の味を、食卓へ —」. Below that, add three short lines of white body copy: 「山椒の痺れと唐辛子の辛さがやみつき！」, 「なめらかな豆腐と旨みたっぷりの肉味噌が絡み合う、」, 「本場の味を再現した自慢の逸品。ご飯が止まらない！辛さ・痺れをお好みでどうぞ。」

The food must look rich, oily, spicy, steaming, and highly appetizing, photographed realistically with shallow depth of field.

Right-side tag: Add one vertical tan paper tag hanging beside the bowl with black Japanese text 「本場の味をそのままに」 and a small red chili pepper illustration at the bottom.

Feature list: On the left side of the bowl, include exactly three circular icon-and-text feature rows separated by dotted gold lines: 1) red chili icon with 「花椒のしびれ」 and subtext 「本場の痺れを再現」, 2) red flame icon with 「唐辛子の辛さ」 and subtext 「ピリ辛が食欲をそそる」, 3) tofu bowl icon with 「なめらか豆腐」 and subtext 「とろける食感と旨み」.

Bottom price band: Add a dark red horizontal band at the bottom. On the left, write 「特別価格」 and a very large cream-gold price {argument name="price" default="1800円"}, followed by 「(税込)」. On the right, create a thin gold ornamental box titled 「おすすめの食べ方」 with text 「温かいご飯にたっぷりかけて、炒め野菜や麺との相性も抜群です。」 and a small bowl-of-rice icon. Along the very bottom edge, include the closing line 「ご家庭で本格四川料理をぜひお楽しみください。」 with gold cloud ornaments.

Visual style: Premium Japanese food poster, bold calligraphy typography, red headline with rough brush texture, cream-gold supporting type, ornamental gold borders, cinematic highlights on the food, authentic Sichuan atmosphere, high contrast, luxurious but rustic.

Constraints: Use exactly one main food bowl, one top-left red seal, one right red seal, one vertical paper tag, three feature icons, one price block, and one recommendation box. Keep all Japanese text legible and placed as described. Avoid extra dishes, extra logos, people, watermarks, or modern UI elements.
```

```
Wardrobe:

Wardrobe assembly rule:
Each FINAL WORN STATE overrides conflicting wearing-state wording only for the item descriptions it explicitly scopes or names, and only for wearing role or use, count, position, side, orientation, routing, fastening, layering, folds, tucking, and draping. Preserve those descriptions for identity, design, material, color, pattern, construction, and proportions.

Top:

One solid white cropped bustier top constructed from fine translucent floral lace and narrow opaque woven binding. The silhouette is close and compact, with a shallow rectangular front envelope, two broad straight straps, and a continuous narrow lower band. A low softly squared neckline spans between the straps. The front is divided into two shallow curved lace panels by a narrow center bridge, with a horizontal opaque binding defining the lower edge of the panels and a second binding completing the base. Dense floral-and-leaf lace fills every framed panel, retaining small irregular transparent cells between the embroidered threads. The broad straps continue into the rear band, where two narrow white tie extensions form one compact bow with two balanced loops and two short finished tails. Keep the lace panels flat and distinct, the binding widths consistent, the strap paths independent, the rear bow compact, and the open lacework crisp rather than blurred into solid white fabric.

One pale sky-blue and white pinstripe longline button-front shirt in fine, lightly crinkled cotton shirting, with dense narrow white stripes running longitudinally across the main panels and sleeves and following the cut direction of the collar, placket, and cuffs. The cut is relaxed and spacious, with dropped shoulders, a straight upper section, and softly expanding volume through the lower section. A broad pointed collar frames a short exposed upper placket that continues into a full center-front opening. Long sleeves follow a generous straight line from low armscyes and narrow into deep rectangular cuffs. Clean side seams, a narrow stitched front placket, and an extended shirttail hem with curved front and rear edges and short side separations complete the panels. Small matte cream four-hole buttons are evenly spaced along the placket. Preserve the fine stripe spacing, crisp pattern alignment, airy shirt volume, clean collar points, controlled cuff structure, and dry woven surface without swollen seams, melted stripes, or fuzzy edges.

Final worn state:
For the second item description above only:
- Front closure: Engage every existing front closure from the top edge to the bottom edge and align the two front edges continuously on the anatomical center front. Leave no open interval, offset panel, or invented fastening. FINAL FASTENING STATE: every selected closed position is physically connected to its matching receiver; every selected open position is visibly disconnected, and panel overlap does not imitate a fastening.
- Sleeves: Release both cuff and sleeve-placket closures, then turn each sleeve outward in broad folds. Position both rolled bands immediately below the elbow points. Preserve both cuffs and keep every fold below the elbows. Do not push either roll above an elbow, hide a cuff, or leave one sleeve at the wrist.
- Layering: Layer this garment underneath the first item description under Top across the upper body regardless of whether either garment has a front opening. Preserve each garment's selected or source-default closure state and expose only the inner garment's neckline, collar, cuffs, sleeves, or hem where the outer garment does not physically cover them. Change only the inside-outside order; do not invent an opening or fastening, merge the garments, or route either garment through the other's neckline, armhole, sleeve, seam, or panel.
- Finish — Natural: Use a few broad soft folds with modest overlap and small large-scale asymmetry while preserving every cuff, hem, seam, and hardware component.

For the second item description under Top and the first item description under Top together only:
- Finish — Relaxed: At the real upper-body overlap at the neckline, armholes, sides, and hems, compress mainly this covered inner garment and let the outer target yield only locally; let only a few broad folds leave those boundaries and descend within each garment's own panels. Preserve both garments' necklines, armholes, straps, sleeves, seams, closures, and hems. Use light stable contact and broader material-correct ease without changing the selected layer order.

Bottom: One charcoal-grey two-tier pleated mini skirt made from lightweight opaque woven fabric with a smooth matte-to-soft-luster face, crisp fold memory, and fluid movement. A clean fitted waistband supports a full upper tier formed from broad regular knife pleats that open into a short circular flare. A second pleated tier is joined beneath the first tier and expands to a slightly deeper lower flare, producing two clearly separated horizontal levels without merging their pleat systems. Every tier edge is finished with one narrow white gathered ruffle tape that remains continuously visible as a bright border against the grey ground. One small muted lavender-grey rectangular woven label is centered on a lower-front outer section, framed by a narrow white scalloped border and carrying the exact two-line inscription “Moonlit” in large white cursive above “Daydream” in smaller white cursive. Keep both tiers evenly distributed, the pleat ridges sharply legible, the white edge trims narrow and continuous, and the rear construction consistent with the same two-tier pleated assembly.

Legwear: One matching pair of warm-ivory knee-high socks, displayed side by side as complete footed products. Opaque fine-rib knit with a soft matte finish, straight tubular shafts, gently shaped heels and toes, and wide plain elasticized cuffs with a subtle folded appearance. Uniform warm-ivory color throughout, tonal linking at the toes, no logos, no stripes, no lace, no graphics.

Footwear: Create one anatomically mirrored matching pair of white low-top lace-up sneakers with rounded shell toes and flat white rubber cupsoles. Build each upper from smooth white leather panels with a low padded collar, seven paired lace positions, flat white laces, a straight central tongue, and a ribbed molded rubber shell covering the complete toe cap. Apply three parallel black stepped bands to the outer quarter, each band formed from two solid diagonal rectangular segments separated by one narrow vertical gap; keep the three bands equal in width and evenly spaced. Add one compact white rectangular tongue label printed with the clearly legible uppercase word “SOLSTICE” in black condensed lettering. Finish the sole with a fine textured sidewall and shallow dark outsole edge, keeping the complete pair identical except for anatomical mirroring, with precise panel stitching, balanced lacing, clean black-white separation, and no graphics outside the confirmed quarter and tongue zones.

Bag:

One compact metallic-silver mini tote bag with a softly structured rectangular body, narrow side gussets, rounded lower corners, and a flat reinforced base. Smooth reflective synthetic leather covers the body with crisp silver edge binding and tonal stitching. Two short rolled top handles attach through rectangular stitched tabs, while one long narrow adjustable strap connects to small silver swivel clasps at the upper side rings and includes a matching slider buckle. A single silver zipper closes the straight top opening and terminates at compact end tabs. Keep the broad faces plain and uninterrupted, with pale-gray lining and no text, patches, appliqués, or decorative ornaments.

Final worn state:
- Bag carry: VIEW: FRONT/NEAR-FRONT SUPPO

### No. 119: Game Asset - Winged Moon Goddess Archer Wallpaper

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

Creates a classical mythological wallpaper by transforming a winged celestial reference figure into a moonlit goddess archer scene.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Using REFERENCE_0 as the classical mythological painting style and winged celestial figure base, transform the subject into {argument name="mythological character" default="a female moon goddess archer inspired by Artemis/Diana"}. Keep the grand Baroque/classical oil-painting treatment, dramatic clouds, monumental feathered wings, flowing pale drapery, sculptural anatomy, and cinematic wide desktop-wallpaper composition, but change the pose so she is aiming a bow in profile with one arrow drawn taut. Add exactly 5 new key details: 1 ornate crescent-shaped bow, 1 nocked arrow, 1 quiver at her waist, 1 crescent moon ornament in her hair, and 1 distant crescent moon in the sky. Shift the lighting and atmosphere from warm heavenly daylight to {argument name="atmosphere" default="dark moonlit night"}, with a starry cloud-filled sky, silver-gray highlights, glowing mist, and ethereal lunar radiance. Make the image feel like a mythological 4K desktop wallpaper: highly detailed, elegant, epic, painterly, no text, no watermark.
```

### No. 120: Game Asset - Emerald Aetheric Surveyor

![Language-EN](https://img.shields.io/badge/Language-EN-blue)
![Raycast](https://img.shields.io/badge/🚀-Raycast_Friendly-purple)

#### 📖 Description

A detailed fantasy concept art prompt for creating an emerald-haired steampunk elven mage with crystal wings and a glowing holographic survey interface.

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Create a vertical 2:3 cinematic fantasy character illustration of {argument name="character name" default="an elven aetheric surveyor"} standing full-body in a grand gothic steampunk observatory. She is a refined young woman with long windswept {argument name="hair color" default="emerald green hair"}, pale skin, pointed ears, elegant serious expression, and detailed anime-realism facial features. Outfit: dark emerald Victorian military coat with gold embroidery and trim, high ruffled cream blouse, fitted black trousers, belts, pouches, brass instruments, black gloves, tall black leather heeled boots with ornate gold buckles, and a long layered cape lined in cream fabric. Behind her are exactly eight large translucent crystalline wing-like shards radiating from her back, faceted and glowing pale mint green like cut glass. In her left hand she holds a tall ornate staff topped with a large green crystal spearhead framed by gold filigree; with her right hand she touches a floating holographic interface panel. The hologram is a luminous green transparent rectangular sci-fi magic screen containing exactly five main information areas: 1) top title text reading {argument name="hologram title" default="AETHERIC SURVEY // LAYER 7"}, 2) an upper-left circular compass/radar diagram, 3) an upper-right irregular map silhouette, 4) a lower-left circular status gauge reading {argument name="status readout" default="STABLE +0.37%"}, and 5) a lower-right analysis card labeled {argument name="analysis label" default="LEYLINE FLUX ANALYSIS"} with small charts and data lines. Background: towering arched windows, dark cathedral columns, brass pipes, gears, mechanical astrolabe machinery, misty volumetric light, polished stone floor with reflections. Color palette of deep green, black, antique gold, pale crystal mint, and smoky gray. Style: highly detailed fantasy concept art, sharp linework blended with painterly realism, luxurious costume design, glowing magical technology, dramatic rim light, depth of field, intricate textures, no watermark, no extra characters.
```

## 🤝 How to Contribute

We welcome contributions! You can submit prompts via:

### 🐛 GitHub Issue

1. Click [**Submit New Prompt**](https://github.com/YouMind-OpenLab/awesome-gpt-image-2/issues/new?template=submit-prompt.yml)
2. Fill in the form with prompt details and image
3. Submit and wait for team review

5. Your prompt will appear in README within 4 hours

**Note:** We only accept submissions via GitHub Issues to ensure quality control.
